```
    16→  operator: any;
    17→  rapport: string;
    18→  notes: string;
    19→  opName: string;
    20→  opQth: string;
    21→  bezirkCode: string;
    22→  suffix?: string;
    23→}
    24→
    25→const SUFFIXES = ['/m', '/p', '/am', '/mm'] as const;
    26→
    27→export default function BundMode({ exerciseId, reports, onReportCreated, onReportUpdated, onReportDeleted }: Props) {
    28→  const [bundeslaender, setBundeslaender] = useState<any[]>([]);
    29→  const [bezirke, setBezirke] = useState<any[]>([]);
    30→  const [linkedRepeaters, setLinkedRepeaters] = useState<any[]>([]);
    31→  const [blRepSelection, setBlRepSelection] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
    32→  const [blOpCallsigns, setBlOpCallsigns] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
    33→  const [blHomeRepeaters, setBlHomeRepeaters] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
    34→  const [collapsedBl, setCollapsedBl] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
    35→  const [addRepBl, setAddRepBl] = useState<string | null>(null);
    36→  const [newRepName, setNewRepName] = useState('');
    37→  const [newRepFreq, setNewRepFreq] = useState('');
    38→  const [newRepCallsign, setNewRepCallsign] = useState('');
    39→  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    40→  const [editForm, setEditForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '', suffix: '' });
    41→  const [dragReportId, setDragReportId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    42→
    43→  useEffect(() => {
    44→    Promise.all([
    45→      apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bundeslaender'),
    46→      apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bezirke'),
    47→      apiFetch('/api/v1/repeaters'),
    48→      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters`),
    49→    ]).then(([bl, bz, allReps, exReps]) => {
    50→      setBundeslaender(bl);
    51→      setBezirke(bz);
    52→      const linked = allReps.filter((r: any) => r.is_linked);
    53→      setLinkedRepeaters(linked);
    54→      // Initialize per-BL defaults: first linked in that BL, else first overall
    55→      const defaults: Record<string, number> = {};
    56→      const ops: Record<string, string> = {};
    57→      const homeReps: Record<string, string> = {};
    58→      for (const b of bl) {
    59→        const inBl = linked.find((r: any) => r.bundesland_code === b.code);
    60→        const repId = inBl ? inBl.id : (linked[0]?.id ?? 0);
    61→        defaults[b.code] = repId;
    62→        // Load existing OP callsign and home repeater from exercise_repeaters
    63→        const exRep = exReps.find((er: any) => er.repeater_id === repId);
    64→        ops[b.code] = exRep?.operator_callsign || '';
    65→        homeReps[b.code] = exRep?.home_repeater || '';
    66→      }
    67→      setBlRepSelection(defaults);
    68→      setBlOpCallsigns(ops);
    69→      setBlHomeRepeaters(homeReps);
    70→    }).catch(() => {});
    71→
    72→    const socket = getSocket();
    73→    const handleOpCallsignUpdated = (data: { exercise_id: string; repeater_id: number; operator_callsign: string }) => {
    74→      if (data.exercise_id !== exerciseId) return;
    75→      setBlRepSelection(sel => {
    76→        for (const [blCode, repId] of Object.entries(sel)) {
    77→          if (repId === data.repeater_id) {
    78→            setBlOpCallsigns(prev => ({ ...prev, [blCode]: data.operator_callsign || '' }));
    79→          }
    80→        }
    81→        return sel;
    82→      });
    83→    };
    84→    const handleHomeRepeaterUpdated = (data: { exercise_id: string; repeater_id: number; home_repeater: string }) => {
    85→      if (data.exercise_id !== exerciseId) return;
    86→      setBlRepSelection(sel => {
    87→        for (const [blCode, repId] of Object.entries(sel)) {
    88→          if (repId === data.repeater_id) {
    89→            setBlHomeRepeaters(prev => ({ ...prev, [blCode]: data.home_repeater || '' }));
    90→          }
    91→        }
    92→        return sel;
    93→      });
    94→    };
    95→    socket.on('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated);
    96→    socket.on('home_repeater_updated', handleHomeRepeaterUpdated);
    97→    return () => {
    98→      socket.off('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated);
    99→      socket.off('home_repeater_updated', handleHomeRepeaterUpdated);
   100→    };
   101→  }, [exerciseId]);
   102→
   103→  const saveOpCallsign = async (blCode: string, val: string) => {
   104→    const repId = blRepSelection[blCode];
   105→    if (!repId) return;
   106→    try {
   107→      // Ensure exercise_repeater exists, then update
   108→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters`, {
   109→        method: 'POST',
   110→        body: JSON.stringify({ repeater_id: repId, operator_callsign: val || null }),
   111→      });
   112→    } catch {
   113→      // Already exists, just patch
   114→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters/${repId}`, {
   115→        method: 'PATCH',
   116→        body: JSON.stringify({ operator_callsign: val || null }),
   117→      });
   118→    }
   119→    getSocket().emit('op_callsign_updated', { exercise_id: exerciseId, repeater_id: repId, operator_callsign: val });
   120→  };
   121→
   122→  const saveHomeRepeater = async (blCode: string, val: string) => {
   123→    const repId = blRepSelection[blCode];
   124→    if (!repId) return;
   125→    try {
   126→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters`, {
   127→        method: 'POST',
   128→        body: JSON.stringify({ repeater_id: repId, home_repeater: val || null }),
   129→      });
   130→    } catch {
   131→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters/${repId}`, {
   132→        method: 'PATCH',
   133→        body: JSON.stringify({ home_repeater: val || null }),
   134→      });
   135→    }
   136→    getSocket().emit('home_repeater_updated', { exercise_id: exerciseId, repeater_id: repId, home_repeater: val });
   137→  };
   138→
   139→  const addRepeater = async (blCode: string) => {
   140→    if (!newRepName && !newRepFreq) return;
   141→    const name = newRepName || `Direkte ${newRepFreq}`;
   142→    const freq = newRepFreq ? parseFloat(newRepFreq) : null;
   143→    try {
   144→      const rep = await apiFetch('/api/v1/repeaters', {
   145→        method: 'POST',
   146→        body: JSON.stringify({
   147→          short_name: name,
   148→          site_name: name,
   149→          frequency_mhz: freq,
   150→          callsign: newRepCallsign || null,
   151→          band: freq ? (freq > 400 ? '70cm' : freq > 200 ? '23cm' : '2m') : null,
   152→          type: 'repeater',
   153→          bundesland_code: blCode,
   154→        }),
   155→      });
   156→      setLinkedRepeaters(prev => [...prev, rep]);
   157→      setNewRepName('');
   158→      setNewRepFreq('');
   159→      setNewRepCallsign('');
   160→      setAddRepBl(null);
   161→    } catch {}
   162→  };
   163→
   164→  const toggleBl = (code: string) => {
   165→    setCollapsedBl(prev => {
   166→      const next = new Set(prev);
   167→      if (next.has(code)) next.delete(code); else next.add(code);
   168→      return next;
   169→    });
   170→  };
   171→
   172→  const parseRapport = (raw: string) => {
   173→    const match = raw.match(/^(\d)\/(\d)(.*)$/);
   174→    if (!match) return { readability: null, strength: null, db_over_s9: null };
   175→    return {
   176→      readability: parseInt(match[1]),
   177→      strength: parseInt(match[2]),
   178→      db_over_s9: match[3]?.trim() || null,
   179→    };
   180→  };
   181→
   182→  const handleBezirkSubmit = async (bezirkCode: string, repeaterId: number, form: EntryForm) => {
   183→    try {
   184→      if (!repeaterId) return;
   185→      const operator = await getOrCreateOperator(form.callsign, form.operator, { name: form.opName, qth: form.opQth });
   186→      if (!operator) return;
   187→
   188→      // Patch name/QTH if user filled them in and they differ from what's stored
   189→      if (operator.id && (form.opName || form.opQth)) {
   190→        const patch: any = {};
   191→        if (form.opName && form.opName !== (operator.name || '')) patch.name = form.opName;
   192→        if (form.opQth && form.opQth !== (operator.qth || '')) patch.qth = form.opQth;
   193→        if (Object.keys(patch).length > 0) {
   194→          await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${operator.id}`, { method: 'PATCH', body: JSON.stringify(patch) });
   195→        }
   196→      }
   197→
   198→      const parsed = parseRapport(form.rapport);
   199→
   200→      const report = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {
   201→        method: 'POST',
   202→        body: JSON.stringify({
   203→          operator_id: operator.id,
   204→          repeater_id: repeaterId,
   205→          ...parsed,
   206→          notes: form.notes || null,
   207→          bezirk_code: (bezirkCode && bezirkCode !== '??') ? bezirkCode : null,
   208→          suffix: form.suffix || null,
   209→        }),
   210→      });
   211→      onReportCreated(report);
   212→    } catch {
   213→    }
   214→  };
   215→
   216→  const handleEdit = (report: any) => {
   217→    setEditingId(report.id);
   218→    setEditForm({
   219→      callsign: report.callsign,
   220→      operator: { id: report.operator_id, callsign: report.callsign, name: report.operator_name || null, qth: report.operator_qth || null },
   221→      rapport: report.readability && report.strength ? `${report.readability}/${report.strength}${report.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '',
   222→      notes: report.notes || '',
   223→      opName: report.operator_name || '',
   224→      opQth: report.operator_qth || '',
   225→      bezirkCode: report.bezirk_code || '',
   226→      suffix: report.suffix || '',
   227→    });
   228→  };
   229→
   230→  const handleUpdate = async () => {
   231→    if (!editingId) return;
   232→    const parsed = parseRapport(editForm.rapport);
   233→    try {
   234→      // Save operator name/qth if changed
   235→      if (editForm.operator?.id) {
   236→        const nameChanged = (editForm.opName || '') !== (editForm.operator.name || '');
   237→        const qthChanged = (editForm.opQth || '') !== (editForm.operator.qth || '');
   238→        if (nameChanged || qthChanged) {
   239→          const patch: any = {};
   240→          if (nameChanged) patch.name = editForm.opName || null;
   241→          if (qthChanged) patch.qth = editForm.opQth || null;
   242→          await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${editForm.operator.id}`, {
   243→            method: 'PATCH',
   244→            body: JSON.stringify(patch),
   245→          });
   246→        }
   247→      }
   248→      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${editingId}`, {
   249→        method: 'PATCH',
   250→        body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: editForm.notes || null, bezirk_code: editForm.bezirkCode || null, suffix: editForm.suffix || null }),
   251→      });
   252→      if (editForm.opName !== undefined) updated.operator_name = editForm.opName || null;
   253→      if (editForm.opQth !== undefined) updated.operator_qth = editForm.opQth || null;
   254→      onReportUpdated(updated);
   255→      setEditingId(null);
   256→    } catch {}
   257→  };
   258→
   259→  const handleMoveToBezirk = async (reportId: number, newBezirkCode: string | null) => {
   260→    try {
   261→      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, {
   262→        method: 'PATCH',
   263→        body: JSON.stringify({ bezirk_code: newBezirkCode }),
   264→      });
   265→      onReportUpdated(updated);
   266→    } catch {}
   267→  };
   268→
   269→  const handleDelete = async (reportId: number) => {
   270→    if (!confirm('Rapport löschen?')) return;
   271→    try {
   272→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
   273→      onReportDeleted(reportId);
   274→    } catch {}
   275→  };
   276→
   277→  const domesticBlCodes = new Set(bundeslaender.filter(b => !b.is_international).map(b => b.code));
   278→
   279→  return (
   280→    <div className="space-y-2">
   281→      {bundeslaender.filter(bl => !bl.is_international).map(bl => {
   282→        const blBezirke = bezirke.filter(b => b.bundesland_code === bl.code);
   283→        const defaultRepId = blRepSelection[bl.code] || 0;
   284→        // Match reports by bezirk's Bundesland, only for linked repeaters
   285→        const blBezirkCodes = new Set(blBezirke.map(b => b.code));
   286→        const blReports = reports.filter(r => {
   287→          if (!r.repeater_is_linked) return false;
   288→          // Primary: match by bezirk code
   289→          if (r.bezirk_code && blBezirkCodes.has(r.bezirk_code)) return true;
   290→          // Fallback for null bezirk_code: use operator BL (if domestic), else repeater BL
   291→          if (!r.bezirk_code) {
   292→            const effectiveBl = domesticBlCodes.has(r.bundesland_code) ? r.bundesland_code : r.repeater_bundesland_code;
   293→            return effectiveBl === bl.code;
   294→          }
   295→          return false;
   296→        });
   297→        const isCollapsed = collapsedBl.has(bl.code);
   298→        const reportCount = blReports.filter(r => !r.is_op_marker && r.readability).length;
   299→
   300→        return (
   301→          <div key={bl.code} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm">
   302→            <div className="bg-gray-50 px-2 sm:px-3 py-2 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between border-b gap-1">
   303→              <div
   304→                onClick={() => toggleBl(bl.code)}
   305→                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100 rounded px-1 -mx-1 min-w-0"
   306→              >
   307→                <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">{isCollapsed ? '▶' : '▼'}</span>
   308→                <span className="font-medium text-xs sm:text-sm truncate">OE{parseInt(bl.code)} {bl.name}</span>
   309→                <span className="bg-[#0d6efd] text-white text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full flex-shrink-0">{reportCount}</span>
   310→              </div>
   311→              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
   312→                <button
   313→                  onClick={() => setAddRepBl(addRepBl === bl.code ? null : bl.code)}
   314→                  className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 hidden sm:inline"
   315→                >
   316→                  + Umsetzer
   317→                </button>
   318→                <label className={`text-xs ${blOpCallsigns[bl.code] ? 'text-gray-500' : 'text-[#c8102e] font-semibold'}`}>OP:</label>
   319→                <input
   320→                  type="text"
   321→                  value={blOpCallsigns[bl.code] || ''}
   322→                  onChange={e => setBlOpCallsigns(prev => ({ ...prev, [bl.code]: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}
   323→                  onBlur={e => saveOpCallsign(bl.code, e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
   324→                  placeholder="Rufz."
   325→                  className={`border rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-mono uppercase w-20 sm:w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 ${blOpCallsigns[bl.code] ? 'border-gray-300' : 'border-[#c8102e] placeholder-[#c8102e]/60'}`}
   326→                />
   327→                <label className="text-xs text-gray-500 hidden sm:inline">REP:</label>
   328→                <select
   329→                  value={blHomeRepeaters[bl.code] || ''}
   330→                  onChange={e => {
   331→                    const val = e.target.value;
   332→                    setBlHomeRepeaters(prev => ({ ...prev, [bl.code]: val }));
   333→                    saveHomeRepeater(bl.code, val);
   334→                  }}
   335→                  className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-24 sm:w-32 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   336→                >
   337→                  <option value="">—</option>
   338→                  {linkedRepeaters.filter(r => r.bundesland_code === bl.code).map(r => (
   339→                    <option key={r.id} value={r.short_name}>{r.site_name || r.short_name}{r.band ? ` ${r.band}` : ''}</option>
   340→                  ))}
   341→                  {linkedRepeaters.filter(r => r.bundesland_code !== bl.code).length > 0 && (
   342→                    <option disabled>──────</option>
   343→                  )}
   344→                  {linkedRepeaters.filter(r => r.bundesland_code !== bl.code).map(r => (
   345→                    <option key={r.id} value={r.short_name}>{r.site_name || r.short_name}{r.band ? ` ${r.band}` : ''}</option>
   346→                  ))}
   347→                </select>
   348→              </div>
   349→            </div>
   350→
   351→            {!isCollapsed && (
   352→              <div>
   353→                {addRepBl === bl.code && (
   354→                  <div className="px-3 py-2 bg-blue-50 border-b flex items-center gap-1 flex-wrap">
   355→                    <input
   356→                      type="text"
   357→                      value={newRepName}
   358→                      onChange={e => setNewRepName(e.target.value)}
   359→                      placeholder="Name"
   360→                      className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-28 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   361→                      autoFocus
   362→                    />
   363→                    <input
   364→                      type="text"
   365→                      value={newRepFreq}
   366→                      onChange={e => setNewRepFreq(e.target.value)}
   367→                      placeholder="MHz"
   368→                      className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-mono w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   369→                    />
   370→                    <input
   371→                      type="text"
   372→                      value={newRepCallsign}
   373→                      onChange={e => setNewRepCallsign(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
   374→                      placeholder="Rufz."
   375→                      className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-mono uppercase w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   376→                    />
   377→                    <button
   378→                      onClick={() => addRepeater(bl.code)}
   379→                      className="bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs"
   380→                    >
   381→                      OK
   382→                    </button>
   383→                    <button
   384→                      onClick={() => { setAddRepBl(null); setNewRepName(''); setNewRepFreq(''); setNewRepCallsign(''); }}
   385→                      className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 text-xs"
   386→                    >
   387→                      Abbrechen
   388→                    </button>
   389→                  </div>
   390→                )}
   391→                <div className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   392→                  {blBezirke.map(bz => {
   393→                    const bzReports = blReports.filter(r => r.bezirk_code === bz.code);
   394→                    return (
   395→                      <BezirkRow
   396→                        key={bz.code}
   397→                        bezirk={bz}
   398→                        reports={bzReports}
   399→                        availableBezirke={blBezirke}
   400→                        allBezirke={bezirke}
   401→                        allBundeslaender={bundeslaender}
   402→                        linkedRepeaters={linkedRepeaters}
   403→                        defaultRepeaterId={defaultRepId}
   404→                        onSubmit={(repeaterId, form) => handleBezirkSubmit(bz.code, repeaterId, form)}
   405→                        onEdit={handleEdit}
   406→                        onDelete={handleDelete}
   407→                        onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   408→                        editingId={editingId}
   409→                        editForm={editForm}
   410→                        onEditChange={setEditForm}
   411→                        onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   412→                        onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   413→                        dragReportId={dragReportId}
   414→                        onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   415→                        onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   416→                      />
   417→                    );
   418→                  })}
   419→                  {/* Sonstige — reports without matching bezirk + entry form */}
   420→                  {(() => {
   421→                    const bzCodes = new Set(blBezirke.map(b => b.code));
   422→                    const unassigned = blReports.filter(r => !r.bezirk_code || !bzCodes.has(r.bezirk_code));
   423→                    return (
   424→                      <BezirkRow
   425→                        bezirk={{ code: '??', name: 'Sonstige', is_capital: false }}
   426→                        reports={unassigned}
   427→                        availableBezirke={blBezirke}
   428→                        allBezirke={bezirke}
   429→                        allBundeslaender={bundeslaender}
   430→                        linkedRepeaters={linkedRepeaters}
   431→                        defaultRepeaterId={defaultRepId}
   432→                        onSubmit={(repeaterId, form) => handleBezirkSubmit('??', repeaterId, form)}
   433→                        onEdit={handleEdit}
   434→                        onDelete={handleDelete}
   435→                        onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   436→                        editingId={editingId}
   437→                        editForm={editForm}
   438→                        onEditChange={setEditForm}
   439→                        onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   440→                        onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   441→                        dragReportId={dragReportId}
   442→                        onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   443→                        onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   444→                      />
   445→                    );
   446→                  })()}
   447→                </div>
   448→              </div>
   449→            )}
   450→          </div>
   451→        );
   452→      })}
   453→    </div>
   454→  );
   455→}
   456→
   457→interface BezirkRowProps {
   458→  bezirk: any;
   459→  reports: any[];
   460→  availableBezirke: any[];
   461→  allBezirke: any[];
   462→  allBundeslaender: any[];
   463→  linkedRepeaters: any[];
   464→  defaultRepeaterId: number;
   465→  onSubmit: (repeaterId: number, form: EntryForm) => void;
   466→  onEdit: (report: any) => void;
   467→  onDelete: (id: number) => void;
   468→  onMoveToBezirk: (reportId: number, bezirkCode: string | null) => void;
   469→  editingId: number | null;
   470→  editForm: EntryForm;
   471→  onEditChange: (form: EntryForm) => void;
   472→  onEditSave: () => void;
   473→  onEditCancel: () => void;
   474→  dragReportId: number | null;
   475→  onDragStart: (reportId: number) => void;
   476→  onDragEnd: () => void;
   477→}
   478→
   479→function BezirkRow({ bezirk, reports, availableBezirke, allBezirke, allBundeslaender, linkedRepeaters, defaultRepeaterId, onSubmit, onEdit, onDelete, onMoveToBezirk, editingId, editForm, onEditChange, onEditSave, onEditCancel, dragReportId, onDragStart, onDragEnd }: BezirkRowProps) {
   480→  const [form, setForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '', suffix: '' });

   585→                      {r.readability && r.strength ? `${r.readability}/${r.strength}${r.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '—'}

   599→      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex items-center gap-1">

   605→          >
```

```
     1→import { useState, useEffect, useRef, type FormEvent } from 'react';
     2→import { apiFetch, getOrCreateOperator } from '../services/api';
     3→import { getSocket } from '../services/socket';

     5→
     6→const BUNDESLAND_NAMES: Record<string, string> = {
     7→  '01': 'OE1 Wien',
     8→  '02': 'OE2 Salzburg',
     9→  '03': 'OE3 Niederösterreich',

    18→
    19→interface Props {

    41→const SUFFIXES = ['/m', '/p', '/am', '/mm'] as const;
    42→
    43→export default function LandMode({ exerciseId, repeaters, reports, simplexBezirke, simplexBlCodes, onReportCreated, onReportUpdated, onReportDeleted }: Props) {
    44→  const [collapsedReps, setCollapsedReps] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
    45→  const [opCallsigns, setOpCallsigns] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
    46→  const [bezirke, setBezirke] = useState<any[]>([]);
    47→  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    48→  const [editForm, setEditForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '', suffix: '' });
    49→  const [dragReportId, setDragReportId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    50→
    51→  // Load bezirke + OP callsigns
    52→  useEffect(() => {
    53→    Promise.all([
    54→      apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bezirke'),
    55→      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters`),
    56→    ]).then(([bz, exReps]) => {
    57→      setBezirke(bz);
    58→      const ops: Record<number, string> = {};
    59→      for (const er of exReps) {
    60→        if (er.operator_callsign) {
    61→          ops[er.repeater_id] = er.operator_callsign;
    62→        }
    63→      }
    64→      setOpCallsigns(ops);
    65→    }).catch(() => {});
    66→
    67→    const socket = getSocket();
    68→    const handleOpCallsignUpdated = (data: { exercise_id: string; repeater_id: number; operator_callsign: string }) => {
    69→      if (data.exercise_id !== exerciseId) return;
    70→      setOpCallsigns(prev => ({ ...prev, [data.repeater_id]: data.operator_callsign || '' }));
    71→    };
    72→    socket.on('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated);
    73→    return () => { socket.off('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated); };
    74→  }, [exerciseId]);
    75→
    76→  // Group repeaters by Bundesland — exclude linked repeaters (those belong to BundMode)
    77→  const repeatersByBl: Record<string, any[]> = {};
    78→  const simplexRepeaters: any[] = [];
    79→  for (const r of repeaters) {
    80→    if (r.is_linked) continue;
    81→    if (r.type === 'simplex') {
    82→      simplexRepeaters.push(r);
    83→    } else {
    84→      const blCode = r.bundesland_code || '_other';
    85→      if (!repeatersByBl[blCode]) repeatersByBl[blCode] = [];
    86→      repeatersByBl[blCode].push(r);
    87→    }
    88→  }
    89→
    90→  const activeBlCodes = simplexBlCodes ? simplexBlCodes.split(',').filter(Boolean) : ['01', '02', '03', '04', '05', '06', '07', '08', '09'];
    91→
    92→  const sortedBlCodes = Object.keys(repeatersByBl).sort((a, b) => {
    93→    if (a === '_other') return 1;
    94→    if (b === '_other') return -1;
    95→    return a.localeCompare(b);
    96→  });
    97→
    98→  const toggleRepeater = (repId: number) => {
    99→    setCollapsedReps(prev => {
   100→      const next = new Set(prev);
   101→      if (next.has(repId)) next.delete(repId); else next.add(repId);
   102→      return next;
   103→    });
   104→  };
   105→
   106→  const saveOpCallsign = async (repeaterId: number, val: string) => {
   107→    try {
   108→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters`, {
   109→        method: 'POST',
   110→        body: JSON.stringify({ repeater_id: repeaterId, operator_callsign: val || null }),
   111→      });
   112→    } catch {
   113→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters/${repeaterId}`, {
   114→        method: 'PATCH',
   115→        body: JSON.stringify({ operator_callsign: val || null }),
   116→      });
   117→    }
   118→    getSocket().emit('op_callsign_updated', { exercise_id: exerciseId, repeater_id: repeaterId, operator_callsign: val });
   119→  };
   120→
   121→  const parseRapport = (raw: string) => {
   122→    const match = raw.match(/^(\d)\/(\d)(.*)$/);
   123→    if (!match) return { readability: null, strength: null, db_over_s9: null };
   124→    return {
   125→      readability: parseInt(match[1]),
   126→      strength: parseInt(match[2]),
   127→      db_over_s9: match[3]?.trim() || null,
   128→    };
   129→  };
   130→
   131→  const handleBezirkSubmit = async (bezirkCode: string, repeaterId: number, form: EntryForm) => {
   132→    try {
   133→      const operator = await getOrCreateOperator(form.callsign, form.operator, { name: form.opName, qth: form.opQth });
   134→      if (!operator) return;
   135→
   136→      // Patch name/QTH if user filled them in and they differ from what's stored
   137→      if (operator.id && (form.opName || form.opQth)) {
   138→        const patch: any = {};
   139→        if (form.opName && form.opName !== (operator.name || '')) patch.name = form.opName;
   140→        if (form.opQth && form.opQth !== (operator.qth || '')) patch.qth = form.opQth;
   141→        if (Object.keys(patch).length > 0) {
   142→          await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${operator.id}`, { method: 'PATCH', body: JSON.stringify(patch) });
   143→        }
   144→      }
   145→
   146→      const parsed = parseRapport(form.rapport);
   147→
   148→      const report = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {
   149→        method: 'POST',
   150→        body: JSON.stringify({
   151→          operator_id: operator.id,
   152→          repeater_id: repeaterId,
   153→          ...parsed,
   154→          notes: form.notes || null,
   155→          bezirk_code: (bezirkCode && bezirkCode !== '??') ? bezirkCode : null,
   156→          suffix: form.suffix || null,
   157→        }),
   158→      });
   159→      onReportCreated(report);
   160→    } catch {}
   161→  };
   162→
   163→  const handleEdit = (report: any) => {
   164→    setEditingId(report.id);
   165→    setEditForm({
   166→      callsign: report.callsign,
   167→      operator: { id: report.operator_id, callsign: report.callsign, name: report.operator_name || null, qth: report.operator_qth || null },
   168→      rapport: report.readability && report.strength ? `${report.readability}/${report.strength}${report.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '',
   169→      notes: report.notes || '',
   170→      opName: report.operator_name || '',
   171→      opQth: report.operator_qth || '',
   172→      bezirkCode: report.bezirk_code || '',
   173→      suffix: report.suffix || '',
   174→    });
   175→  };
   176→
   177→  const handleUpdate = async () => {
   178→    if (!editingId) return;
   179→    const parsed = parseRapport(editForm.rapport);
   180→    try {
   181→      // Save operator name/qth if changed
   182→      if (editForm.operator?.id) {
   183→        const nameChanged = (editForm.opName || '') !== (editForm.operator.name || '');
   184→        const qthChanged = (editForm.opQth || '') !== (editForm.operator.qth || '');
   185→        if (nameChanged || qthChanged) {
   186→          const patch: any = {};
   187→          if (nameChanged) patch.name = editForm.opName || null;
   188→          if (qthChanged) patch.qth = editForm.opQth || null;
   189→          await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${editForm.operator.id}`, {
   190→            method: 'PATCH',
   191→            body: JSON.stringify(patch),
   192→          });
   193→        }
   194→      }
   195→      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${editingId}`, {
   196→        method: 'PATCH',
   197→        body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: editForm.notes || null, bezirk_code: editForm.bezirkCode || null, suffix: editForm.suffix || null }),
   198→      });
   199→      // Update the returned report with new name/qth so display refreshes
   200→      if (editForm.opName !== undefined) updated.operator_name = editForm.opName || null;
   201→      if (editForm.opQth !== undefined) updated.operator_qth = editForm.opQth || null;
   202→      onReportUpdated(updated);
   203→      setEditingId(null);
   204→    } catch {}
   205→  };
   206→
   207→  const handleMoveToBezirk = async (reportId: number, newBezirkCode: string | null) => {
   208→    try {
   209→      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, {
   210→        method: 'PATCH',
   211→        body: JSON.stringify({ bezirk_code: newBezirkCode }),
   212→      });
   213→      onReportUpdated(updated);
   214→    } catch {}
   215→  };
   216→
   217→  const handleDelete = async (reportId: number) => {
   218→    if (!confirm('Rapport löschen?')) return;
   219→    try {
   220→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
   221→      onReportDeleted(reportId);
   222→    } catch {}
   223→  };
   224→
   225→  const renderRepeaterCard = (rep: any) => {
   226→    const repReports = reports.filter(r => r.repeater_id === rep.repeater_id && !r.is_op_marker);
   227→    const reportCount = repReports.filter(r => r.readability).length;
   228→    const isCollapsed = collapsedReps.has(rep.repeater_id);
   229→    const isSimplexGrouped = simplexBezirke && rep.type === 'simplex';
   230→    const repBezirke = isSimplexGrouped
   231→      ? bezirke.filter(b => activeBlCodes.includes(b.bundesland_code))
   232→      : bezirke.filter(b => b.bundesland_code === rep.bundesland_code);
   233→
   234→    // Group bezirke by BL for simplex grouped rendering
   235→    const bezirkeByBl: Record<string, any[]> = {};
   236→    if (isSimplexGrouped) {
   237→      for (const bz of repBezirke) {
   238→        if (!bezirkeByBl[bz.bundesland_code]) bezirkeByBl[bz.bundesland_code] = [];
   239→        bezirkeByBl[bz.bundesland_code].push(bz);
   240→      }
   241→    }
   242→
   243→    return (
   244→      <div key={rep.repeater_id} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm">
   245→        <div className="bg-gray-50 px-2 sm:px-3 py-2 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between border-b gap-1">
   246→          <div
   247→            onClick={() => toggleRepeater(rep.repeater_id)}
   248→            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100 rounded px-1 -mx-1 min-w-0"
   249→          >
   250→            <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">{isCollapsed ? '▶' : '▼'}</span>
   251→            <span className="font-medium text-xs sm:text-sm truncate">{rep.short_name}{rep.repeater_callsign ? ` (${rep.repeater_callsign})` : ''}</span>
   252→            <span className="bg-[#0d6efd] text-white text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full flex-shrink-0">{reportCount}</span>
   253→            {rep.frequency_mhz && (
   254→              <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 hidden sm:inline">{rep.frequency_mhz} MHz</span>
   255→            )}
   256→            {rep.offset_mhz && (
   257→              <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 hidden md:inline">({rep.offset_mhz > 0 ? '+' : ''}{rep.offset_mhz})</span>
   258→            )}
   259→            {rep.ctcss_hz && (
   260→              <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 hidden md:inline">CTCSS {rep.ctcss_hz}</span>
   261→            )}
   262→          </div>
   263→          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
   264→            <label className={`text-xs ${opCallsigns[rep.repeater_id] ? 'text-gray-500' : 'text-[#c8102e] font-semibold'}`}>OP:</label>
   265→            <input
   266→              type="text"
   267→              value={opCallsigns[rep.repeater_id] || ''}
   268→              onChange={e => setOpCallsigns(prev => ({ ...prev, [rep.repeater_id]: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}
   269→              onBlur={e => saveOpCallsign(rep.repeater_id, e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
   270→              placeholder="Rufz."
   271→              className={`border rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-mono uppercase w-20 sm:w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 ${opCallsigns[rep.repeater_id] ? 'border-gray-300' : 'border-[#c8102e] placeholder-[#c8102e]/60'}`}
   272→            />
   273→          </div>
   274→        </div>
   275→
   276→        {!isCollapsed && (
   277→          <div className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   278→            {repBezirke.length > 0 ? (
   279→              <>
   280→                {isSimplexGrouped ? (
   281→                  /* Simplex grouped by BL → Bezirk */
   282→                  <>
   283→                    {activeBlCodes.sort().map(blCode => {
   284→                      const blBezirke = bezirkeByBl[blCode] || [];
   285→                      if (blBezirke.length === 0) return null;
   286→                      const blLabel = BUNDESLAND_NAMES[blCode] || blCode;
   287→                      return (
   288→                        <div key={blCode}>
   289→                          <div className="bg-gray-100 px-2 sm:px-3 py-1">
   290→                            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">{blLabel}</span>
   291→                          </div>
   292→                          {blBezirke.map(bz => {
   293→                            const bzReports = repReports.filter(r => r.bezirk_code === bz.code);
   294→                            return (
   295→                              <BezirkRow
   296→                                key={bz.code}
   297→                                bezirk={bz}
   298→                                reports={bzReports}
   299→                                repeaterId={rep.repeater_id}
   300→                                availableBezirke={repBezirke}
   301→                                onSubmit={(form) => handleBezirkSubmit(bz.code, rep.repeater_id, form)}
   302→                                onEdit={handleEdit}
   303→                                onDelete={handleDelete}
   304→                                onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   305→                                editingId={editingId}
   306→                                editForm={editForm}
   307→                                onEditChange={setEditForm}
   308→                                onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   309→                                onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   310→                                dragReportId={dragReportId}
   311→                                onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   312→                                onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   313→                              />
   314→                            );
   315→                          })}
   316→                        </div>
   317→                      );
   318→                    })}
   319→                    {/* Sonstige — reports without matching bezirk */}
   320→                    {(() => {
   321→                      const bzCodes = new Set(repBezirke.map(b => b.code));
   322→                      const unassigned = repReports.filter(r => !r.bezirk_code || !bzCodes.has(r.bezirk_code));
   323→                      return (
   324→                        <BezirkRow
   325→                          bezirk={{ code: '??', name: 'Sonstige', is_capital: false }}
   326→                          reports={unassigned}
   327→                          repeaterId={rep.repeater_id}
   328→                          availableBezirke={repBezirke}
   329→                          onSubmit={(form) => handleBezirkSubmit('??', rep.repeater_id, form)}
   330→                          onEdit={handleEdit}
   331→                          onDelete={handleDelete}
   332→                          onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   333→                          editingId={editingId}
   334→                          editForm={editForm}
   335→                          onEditChange={setEditForm}
   336→                          onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   337→                          onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   338→                          dragReportId={dragReportId}
   339→                          onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   340→                          onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   341→                        />
   342→                      );
   343→                    })()}
   344→                  </>
   345→                ) : (
   346→                  /* Normal repeater with bezirke */
   347→                  <>
   348→                    {repBezirke.map(bz => {
   349→                      const bzReports = repReports.filter(r => r.bezirk_code === bz.code);
   350→                      return (
   351→                        <BezirkRow
   352→                          key={bz.code}
   353→                          bezirk={bz}
   354→                          reports={bzReports}
   355→                          repeaterId={rep.repeater_id}
   356→                          availableBezirke={repBezirke}
   357→                          onSubmit={(form) => handleBezirkSubmit(bz.code, rep.repeater_id, form)}
   358→                          onEdit={handleEdit}
   359→                          onDelete={handleDelete}
   360→                          onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   361→                          editingId={editingId}
   362→                          editForm={editForm}
   363→                          onEditChange={setEditForm}
   364→                          onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   365→                          onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   366→                          dragReportId={dragReportId}
   367→                          onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   368→                          onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   369→                        />
   370→                      );
   371→                    })}
   372→                    {/* Sonstige — reports without matching bezirk + entry form */}
   373→                    {(() => {
   374→                      const bzCodes = new Set(repBezirke.map(b => b.code));
   375→                      const unassigned = repReports.filter(r => !r.bezirk_code || !bzCodes.has(r.bezirk_code));
   376→                      return (
   377→                        <BezirkRow
   378→                          bezirk={{ code: '??', name: 'Sonstige', is_capital: false }}
   379→                          reports={unassigned}
   380→                          repeaterId={rep.repeater_id}
   381→                          availableBezirke={repBezirke}
   382→                          onSubmit={(form) => handleBezirkSubmit('??', rep.repeater_id, form)}
   383→                          onEdit={handleEdit}
   384→                          onDelete={handleDelete}
   385→                          onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   386→                          editingId={editingId}
   387→                          editForm={editForm}
   388→                          onEditChange={setEditForm}
   389→                          onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   390→                          onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   391→                          dragReportId={dragReportId}
   392→                          onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   393→                          onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   394→                        />
   395→                      );
   396→                    })()}
   397→                  </>
   398→                )}
   399→              </>
   400→            ) : (
   401→              /* No Bezirke — flat list with input form */
   402→              <FlatReportRow
   403→                reports={repReports}
   404→                repeaterId={rep.repeater_id}
   405→                onSubmit={(form) => handleBezirkSubmit('', rep.repeater_id, form)}
   406→                onEdit={handleEdit}
   407→                onDelete={handleDelete}
   408→                editingId={editingId}
   409→                editForm={editForm}
   410→                onEditChange={setEditForm}
   411→                onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   412→                onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   413→              />
   414→            )}
   415→          </div>
   416→        )}
   417→      </div>
   418→    );
   419→  };
   420→
   421→  return (
   422→    <div className="space-y-2">
   423→      {sortedBlCodes.map(blCode => {
   424→        const blReps = repeatersByBl[blCode];
   425→        const label = BUNDESLAND_NAMES[blCode] || 'Sonstige';
   426→        return (
   427→          <div key={blCode}>
   428→            <div className="px-1 mb-1 mt-2">
   429→              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">{label}</span>
   430→            </div>
   431→            <div className="space-y-2">
   432→              {blReps.map(rep => renderRepeaterCard(rep))}
   433→            </div>
   434→          </div>
   435→        );
   436→      })}
   437→
   438→      {simplexRepeaters.length > 0 && (
   439→        <div>
   440→          <div className="px-1 mb-1 mt-2">
   441→            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">Simplex</span>
   442→          </div>
   443→          <div className="space-y-2">
   444→            {simplexRepeaters.map(rep => renderRepeaterCard(rep))}
   445→          </div>
   446→        </div>
   447→      )}
   448→    </div>
   449→  );
   450→}
   451→
   452→interface BezirkRowProps {
   453→  bezirk: any;
   454→  reports: any[];
   455→  repeaterId: number;
   456→  availableBezirke: any[];
   457→  onSubmit: (form: EntryForm) => void;
   458→  onEdit: (report: any) => void;
   459→  onDelete: (id: number) => void;
   460→  onMoveToBezirk: (reportId: number, bezirkCode: string | null) => void;
   461→  editingId: number | null;
   462→  editForm: EntryForm;
   463→  onEditChange: (form: EntryForm) => void;
   464→  onEditSave: () => void;
   465→  onEditCancel: () => void;
   466→  dragReportId: number | null;
   467→  onDragStart: (reportId: number) => void;
   468→  onDragEnd: () => void;
   469→}
   470→
   471→function BezirkRow({ bezirk, reports, repeaterId, availableBezirke, onSubmit, onEdit, onDelete, onMoveToBezirk, editingId, editForm, onEditChange, onEditSave, onEditCancel, dragReportId, onDragStart, onDragEnd }: BezirkRowProps) {
   472→  const [form, setForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '', suffix: '' });
   473→  const [dragOver, setDragOver] = useState(false);
   474→  const callsignRef = useRef<CallsignInputRef>(null);
   475→  const rapportRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   476→
   477→  const handleSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {
   478→    e.preventDefault();
   479→    if (!form.callsign && !form.operator) return;
   480→    onSubmit(form);
   481→    setForm({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '', suffix: '' });
   482→    setTimeout(() => callsignRef.current?.focus(), 50);
   483→  };
   484→
   485→  const handleDrop = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
   486→    e.preventDefault();
   487→    setDragOver(false);
   488→    if (dragReportId) {
   489→      const targetCode = bezirk.code === '??' ? null : bezirk.code;
   490→      onMoveToBezirk(dragReportId, targetCode);
   491→    }
   492→  };
   493→
   494→  const saveOperatorField = (field: 'name' | 'qth', value: string) => {
   495→    if (!form.operator?.id) return;
   496→    const original = field === 'name' ? (form.operator.name || '') : (form.operator.qth || '');
   497→    if (value === original) return;
   498→    apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${form.operator.id}`, {
   499→      method: 'PATCH',
   500→      body: JSON.stringify({ [field]: value || null }),
   501→    }).then(() => {
   502→      setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: { ...f.operator, [field]: value || null } }));
   503→    }).catch(() => {});
   504→  };
   505→
   506→  return (
   507→    <div
   508→      className={`px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors ${dragOver && dragReportId ? 'bg-blue-100 ring-2 ring-blue-400 ring-inset' : ''}`}
   509→      onDragOver={e => { if (dragReportId) { e.preventDefault(); setDragOver(true); } }}
   510→      onDragLeave={() => setDragOver(false)}
   511→      onDrop={handleDrop}
   512→    >
   513→      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 mb-1">
   514→        <span className={`text-xs font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-white ${bezirk.code === '??' ? 'bg-[#ffc107] text-gray-800' : bezirk.is_capital ? 'bg-[#dc3545]' : 'bg-[#6c757d]'}`}>
   515→          {bezirk.code === '??' ? '??' : bezirk.code}
   516→        </span>
   517→        <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 truncate">{bezirk.name}</span>
   518→        {reports.length > 0 && <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0">({reports.length})</span>}
   519→      </div>
   520→
   521→      {reports.length > 0 && (
   522→        <table className="w-full text-xs mb-1">
   523→          <tbody>
   524→            {reports.map((r, idx) => (
   525→              <tr
   526→                key={r.id}
   527→                className={`hover:bg-blue-50 group ${dragReportId === r.id ? 'opacity-40' : ''}`}
   528→                draggable={editingId !== r.id}
   529→                onDragStart={e => { e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move'; onDragStart(r.id); }}
   530→                onDragEnd={onDragEnd}
   531→              >
   532→                {editingId === r.id ? (
   533→                  <td colSpan={5} className="py-0.5">
   534→                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-blue-50 rounded p-1 flex-wrap" onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); onEditSave(); } }}>
   535→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}</span>
   536→                      <input value={editForm.opName} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opName: e.target.value })} placeholder="Name" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 hidden sm:block" />
   537→                      <input value={editForm.opQth} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opQth: e.target.value })} placeholder="QTH" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-14 sm:w-16 hidden sm:block" />
   538→                      <input value={editForm.rapport} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" autoFocus />
   539→                      <input value={editForm.notes} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, notes: e.target.value })} placeholder="Sonst." className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" />
   540→                      <select
   541→                        value={editForm.bezirkCode}
   542→                        onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, bezirkCode: e.target.value })}
   543→                        className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-14 sm:w-16"
   544→                        title="Bezirk"
   545→                      >
   546→                        <option value="">??</option>
   547→                        {availableBezirke.map(bz => <option key={bz.code} value={bz.code}>{bz.code}</option>)}
   548→                      </select>
   549→                      <button onClick={onEditSave} className="text-green-600 text-xs font-bold">OK</button>
   550→                      <button onClick={onEditCancel} className="text-gray-400 text-xs">X</button>
   551→                    </div>
   552→                  </td>
   553→                ) : (
   554→                  <>
   555→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-400 w-4 cursor-grab">{idx + 1}</td>
   556→                    <td className="py-0.5 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   557→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}{r.suffix || ''}</span>
   558→                      {r.operator_name && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-500 text-xs">{r.operator_name}</span>}
   559→                      {r.operator_qth && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-400 text-xs hidden sm:inline">{r.operator_qth}</span>}
   560→                    </td>
   561→                    <td className="py-0.5 font-mono text-gray-500 w-16 sm:w-24 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   562→                      {r.readability && r.strength ? `${r.readability}/${r.strength}${r.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '—'}
   563→                    </td>
   564→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-500 cursor-pointer hidden sm:table-cell" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>{r.notes || ''}</td>
   565→                    <td className="py-0.5 w-4">
   566→                      <button onClick={() => onDelete(r.id)} className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100">x</button>
   567→                    </td>
   568→                  </>
   569→                )}
   570→              </tr>
   571→            ))}
   572→          </tbody>
   573→        </table>
   574→      )}
   575→
   576→      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex items-center gap-1">
   577→        <CallsignInput
   578→          ref={callsignRef}
   579→          value={form.callsign}
   580→          onChange={v => setForm(f => ({ ...f, callsign: v, operator: null, opName: '', opQth: '' }))}
   581→          onSelect={op => {
   582→            setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: op, callsign: op.callsign, opName: op.name || '', opQth: op.qth || '' }));
   583→            setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50);
   584→          }}
   585→          onEnter={() => setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50)}
   586→          className="w-24 sm:w-28"
   587→        />
   588→        <div className="flex gap-0.5">
   589→          {SUFFIXES.map(s => (
   590→            <button
   591→              key={s}
   592→              type="button"
   593→              onClick={() => setForm(f => ({ ...f, suffix: f.suffix === s ? '' : s }))}
   594→              className={`px-1 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-mono border ${form.suffix === s ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-600' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-200'}`}
   595→              title={s}
   596→            >
   597→              {s.slice(1)}
   598→            </button>
   599→          ))}
   600→        </div>
   601→        <input
   602→          type="text"
   603→          value={form.opName}
   604→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opName: e.target.value }))}
   605→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('name', e.target.value)}
   606→          placeholder="Name"
   607→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-20 sm:w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   608→        />
   609→        <input
   610→          type="text"
   611→          value={form.opQth}
   612→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opQth: e.target.value }))}
   613→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('qth', e.target.value)}
   614→          placeholder="QTH"
   615→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   616→        />
   617→        <input
   618→          ref={rapportRef}
   619→          type="text"
   620→          value={form.rapport}
   621→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}
   622→          placeholder="5/9"
   623→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   624→          onKeyDown={e => {
   625→            if (e.altKey && e.key >= '1' && e.key <= '9') {
   626→              e.preventDefault();
   627→              setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: `5/${e.key}` }));
   628→            }
   629→          }}
   630→        />
   631→        <input
   632→          type="text"
   633→          value={form.notes}
   634→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, notes: e.target.value }))}
   635→          placeholder="Sonst."
   636→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs flex-1 min-w-[40px] sm:min-w-[60px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   637→        />
   638→        <button type="submit" title="Rapport speichern (Enter)" className="bg-[#c8102e] hover:bg-[#a00d24] text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs flex-shrink-0">
   639→          + ↵
   640→        </button>
   641→      </form>
   642→    </div>
   643→  );
   644→}
   645→
   646→interface FlatReportRowProps {
   647→  reports: any[];
   648→  repeaterId: number;
   649→  onSubmit: (form: EntryForm) => void;
   650→  onEdit: (report: any) => void;
   651→  onDelete: (id: number) => void;
   652→  editingId: number | null;
   653→  editForm: EntryForm;
   654→  onEditChange: (form: EntryForm) => void;
   655→  onEditSave: () => void;
   656→  onEditCancel: () => void;
   657→}
   658→
   659→function FlatReportRow({ reports, repeaterId, onSubmit, onEdit, onDelete, editingId, editForm, onEditChange, onEditSave, onEditCancel }: FlatReportRowProps) {
   660→  const [form, setForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '' });
   661→  const callsignRef = useRef<CallsignInputRef>(null);
   662→  const rapportRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   663→
   664→  const handleSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {
   665→    e.preventDefault();
   666→    if (!form.callsign && !form.operator) return;
   667→    onSubmit(form);
   668→    setForm({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '' });
   669→    setTimeout(() => callsignRef.current?.focus(), 50);
   670→  };
   671→
   672→  const saveOperatorField = (field: 'name' | 'qth', value: string) => {
   673→    if (!form.operator?.id) return;
   674→    const original = field === 'name' ? (form.operator.name || '') : (form.operator.qth || '');
   675→    if (value === original) return;
   676→    apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${form.operator.id}`, {
   677→      method: 'PATCH',
   678→      body: JSON.stringify({ [field]: value || null }),
   679→    }).then(() => {
   680→      setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: { ...f.operator, [field]: value || null } }));
   681→    }).catch(() => {});
   682→  };
   683→
   684→  return (
   685→    <div className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5">
   686→      {reports.length > 0 && (
   687→        <table className="w-full text-xs mb-1">
   688→          <tbody>
   689→            {reports.map((r, idx) => (
   690→              <tr key={r.id} className="hover:bg-blue-50 group">
   691→                {editingId === r.id ? (
   692→                  <td colSpan={5} className="py-0.5">
   693→                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-blue-50 rounded p-1 flex-wrap" onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); onEditSave(); } }}>
   694→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}</span>
   695→                      <input value={editForm.opName} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opName: e.target.value })} placeholder="Name" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 hidden sm:block" />
   696→                      <input value={editForm.opQth} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opQth: e.target.value })} placeholder="QTH" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-14 sm:w-16 hidden sm:block" />
   697→                      <input value={editForm.rapport} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" autoFocus />
   698→                      <input value={editForm.notes} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, notes: e.target.value })} placeholder="Sonst." className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" />
   699→                      <button onClick={onEditSave} className="text-green-600 text-xs font-bold">OK</button>
   700→                      <button onClick={onEditCancel} className="text-gray-400 text-xs">X</button>
   701→                    </div>
   702→                  </td>
   703→                ) : (
   704→                  <>
   705→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-400 w-4">{idx + 1}</td>
   706→                    <td className="py-0.5 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   707→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}{r.suffix || ''}</span>
   708→                      {r.operator_name && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-500 text-xs">{r.operator_name}</span>}
   709→                      {r.operator_qth && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-400 text-xs hidden sm:inline">{r.operator_qth}</span>}
   710→                    </td>
   711→                    <td className="py-0.5 font-mono text-gray-500 w-16 sm:w-24 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   712→                      {r.readability && r.strength ? `${r.readability}/${r.strength}${r.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '—'}
   713→                    </td>
   714→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-500 cursor-pointer hidden sm:table-cell" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>{r.notes || ''}</td>
   715→                    <td className="py-0.5 w-4">
   716→                      <button onClick={() => onDelete(r.id)} className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100">x</button>
   717→                    </td>
   718→                  </>
   719→                )}
   720→              </tr>
   721→            ))}
   722→          </tbody>
   723→        </table>
   724→      )}
   725→
   726→      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex items-center gap-1">
   727→        <CallsignInput
   728→          ref={callsignRef}
   729→          value={form.callsign}
   730→          onChange={v => setForm(f => ({ ...f, callsign: v, operator: null, opName: '', opQth: '' }))}
   731→          onSelect={op => {
   732→            setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: op, callsign: op.callsign, opName: op.name || '', opQth: op.qth || '' }));
   733→            setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50);
   734→          }}
   735→          onEnter={() => setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50)}
   736→          className="w-24 sm:w-28"
   737→        />
   738→        <div className="flex gap-0.5">
   739→          {SUFFIXES.map(s => (
   740→            <button
   741→              key={s}
   742→              type="button"
   743→              onClick={() => setForm(f => ({ ...f, suffix: f.suffix === s ? '' : s }))}
   744→              className={`px-1 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-mono border ${form.suffix === s ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-600' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-200'}`}
   745→              title={s}
   746→            >
   747→              {s.slice(1)}
   748→            </button>
   749→          ))}
   750→        </div>
   751→        <input
   752→          type="text"
   753→          value={form.opName}
   754→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opName: e.target.value }))}
   755→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('name', e.target.value)}
   756→          placeholder="Name"
   757→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-20 sm:w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   758→        />
   759→        <input
   760→          type="text"
   761→          value={form.opQth}
   762→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opQth: e.target.value }))}
   763→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('qth', e.target.value)}
   764→          placeholder="QTH"
   765→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   766→        />
   767→        <input
   768→          ref={rapportRef}
   769→          type="text"
   770→          value={form.rapport}
   771→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}
   772→          placeholder="5/9"
   773→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   774→          onKeyDown={e => {
   775→            if (e.altKey && e.key >= '1' && e.key <= '9') {
   776→              e.preventDefault();
   777→              setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: `5/${e.key}` }));
   778→            }
   779→          }}
   780→        />
   781→        <input
   782→          type="text"
   783→          value={form.notes}
   784→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, notes: e.target.value }))}
   785→          placeholder="Sonst."
   786→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs flex-1 min-w-[40px] sm:min-w-[60px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   787→        />
   788→        <button type="submit" title="Rapport speichern (Enter)" className="bg-[#c8102e] hover:bg-[#a00d24] text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs flex-shrink-0">
   789→          + ↵
   790→        </button>
   791→      </form>
   792→    </div>
   793→  );
   794→}
   795→
```

```
    33→
    34→export default function EmHamImport({ exerciseId, onClose, onImported }: Props) {
    35→  const [step, setStep] = useState<'upload' | 'preview' | 'done'>('upload');
    36→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    37→  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    38→  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<PreviewData | null>(null);
    39→  const [filename, setFilename] = useState<string | null>(null);
    40→  const [importResult, setImportResult] = useState<{ created: number; skipped: number; duplicates: number } | null>(null);
    41→
    42→  const handleUpload = useCallback(async (file: File) => {
    43→    setLoading(true);
    44→    setError(null);
    45→    try {
    46→      const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    47→      const formData = new FormData();
    48→      formData.append('file', file);
    49→      const res = await fetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/import-emham`, {
    50→        method: 'POST',
    51→        headers: { ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}) },
    52→        body: formData,
    53→      });
    54→      if (res.status === 401) {
    55→        localStorage.removeItem('token');
    56→        localStorage.removeItem('admin');
    57→        window.location.href = '/login';
    58→        return;
    59→      }
    60→      if (!res.ok) {
    61→        const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({ error: 'Fehler' }));
    62→        throw new Error(err.error || `HTTP ${res.status}`);
    63→      }
    64→      const data: PreviewData = await res.json();
    65→      setPreview(data);
    66→      setFilename(file.name);
    67→      setStep('preview');
    68→    } catch (err: any) {
    69→      setError(err.message || 'Upload fehlgeschlagen');
    70→    } finally {
    71→      setLoading(false);
    72→    }
    73→  }, [exerciseId]);
    74→
    75→  const handleConfirm = useCallback(async () => {
    76→    if (!preview) return;
    77→    setLoading(true);
    78→    setError(null);
    79→    try {
    80→      const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    81→      const res = await fetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/import-emham/confirm`, {
    82→        method: 'POST',
    83→        headers: {
    84→          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    85→          ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
    86→        },
    87→        body: JSON.stringify({ rows: preview.rows, filename }),
    88→      });
    89→      if (!res.ok) {
    90→        const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({ error: 'Fehler' }));
    91→        throw new Error(err.error || `HTTP ${res.status}`);
    92→      }
    93→      const result = await res.json();
    94→      setImportResult({ created: result.created, skipped: result.skipped, duplicates: result.duplicates });
    95→      onImported(result.reports || []);
    96→      setStep('done');
    97→    } catch (err: any) {
    98→      setError(err.message || 'Import fehlgeschlagen');
    99→    } finally {
   100→      setLoading(false);
   101→    }
   102→  }, [exerciseId, preview, filename, onImported]);
   103→
   104→  const importableCount = preview?.rows.filter(r => r.matched_repeater_id && !r.is_duplicate).length ?? 0;
   105→  const duplicateCount = preview?.rows.filter(r => r.is_duplicate).length ?? 0;
   106→  const issueCount = preview?.rows.filter(r => r.issues.length > 0).length ?? 0;
   107→

   111→  }

   115→      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-xl max-w-4xl w-full max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
   116→        <div className="p-4 border-b flex items-center justify-between">
   117→          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-[#1e3a5f]">EmHam PDF Import</h2>
   118→          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 text-xl leading-none">&times;</button>
   119→        </div>
   120→

   122→          {error && (

   124→              {error}
   125→            </div>

   127→
   128→          {step === 'upload' && (

   133→                <input

   135→                  accept=".pdf"

   137→                  disabled={loading}
   138→                  onChange={e => {

   140→                    if (file) handleUpload(file);

   142→                />
   143→              </label>
   144→              <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-3">Nur .pdf Dateien, max. 10 MB</p>

   147→
   148→          {step === 'preview' && preview && (

   175→                        </td>
```

```
     1→#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2→set -e
     3→
     4→# Colors for output
     5→RED='\033[0;31m'
     6→GREEN='\033[0;32m'
     7→YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
     8→NC='\033[0m' # No Color
     9→
    10→# Load environment variables
    11→if [ -f ".env.production" ]; then
    12→    source .env.production
    13→else
    14→    echo -e "${RED}Error: .env.production file not found${NC}"
    15→    echo "Copy .env.production.example to .env.production and configure it"
    16→    exit 1
    17→fi
    18→
    19→# Validate required variables
    20→REQUIRED_VARS="DEPLOY_HOST REMOTE_DIR CONTAINER_NAME IMAGE_NAME CONTAINER_PORT SITE_URL JWT_SECRET"
    21→for var in $REQUIRED_VARS; do
    22→    if [ -z "${!var}" ]; then
    23→        echo -e "${RED}Error: $var is not set in .env.production${NC}"
    24→        exit 1
    25→    fi
    26→done
    27→
    28→# Check for --rebuild flag
    29→REBUILD_FLAG=""
    30→if [ "$1" == "--rebuild" ]; then
    31→    REBUILD_FLAG="--no-cache"
    32→    echo -e "${YELLOW}Rebuild flag set — will build without cache${NC}"
    33→fi
    34→
    35→echo -e "${GREEN}=== Deploying BOS-ARSA Log to $DEPLOY_HOST ===${NC}"
    36→echo "Host: $DEPLOY_HOST"
    37→echo "Remote dir: $REMOTE_DIR"
    38→echo "Container: $CONTAINER_NAME"
    39→echo "Port: $CONTAINER_PORT"
    40→echo ""
    41→
    42→# Step 1: Sync code to remote host
    43→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 1: Syncing code...${NC}"
    44→GIT_URL=$(git remote get-url origin 2>/dev/null || echo "")
    45→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "
    46→    if [ -d '$REMOTE_DIR' ]; then
    47→        cd '$REMOTE_DIR' && git pull
    48→    else
    49→        mkdir -p '$(dirname $REMOTE_DIR)'
    50→        git clone '$GIT_URL' '$REMOTE_DIR'
    51→    fi
    52→"
    53→
    54→# Step 2: Copy .env for docker compose
    55→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 2: Copying environment config...${NC}"
    56→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "cat > '$REMOTE_DIR/.env' << 'ENVEOF'
    57→JWT_SECRET=$JWT_SECRET
    58→ENVEOF"
    59→
    60→# Step 3: Build Docker image on remote
    61→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 3: Building Docker image...${NC}"
    62→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "
    63→    cd '$REMOTE_DIR'
    64→    docker build $REBUILD_FLAG -t '$IMAGE_NAME' .
    65→"
    66→
    67→# Step 4: Restart container (volume is preserved — data persists)
    68→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 4: Restarting container...${NC}"
    69→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "
    70→    cd '$REMOTE_DIR'
    71→    docker compose up -d --force-recreate --build
    72→"
    73→
    74→# Step 5: Verify deployment
    75→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 5: Verifying deployment...${NC}"
    76→sleep 3
    77→
    78→# Check container status
    79→CONTAINER_STATUS=$(ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "docker ps --filter 'name=$CONTAINER_NAME' --format '{{.Status}}'")
    80→echo "Container status: $CONTAINER_STATUS"
    81→
    82→# Check local port
    83→LOCAL_PORT=$(echo $CONTAINER_PORT | cut -d: -f1)
    84→if ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "curl -sf -o /dev/null http://localhost:$LOCAL_PORT/api/v1/auth/login"; then
    85→    echo -e "${GREEN}✓ Container is running on port $LOCAL_PORT${NC}"
    86→else
    87→    echo -e "${YELLOW}⚠ Container not yet responding on port $LOCAL_PORT — may still be starting${NC}"
    88→fi
    89→
    90→# Check public URL
    91→if curl -sf -o /dev/null "$SITE_URL" 2>/dev/null; then
    92→    echo -e "${GREEN}✓ Site is accessible at $SITE_URL${NC}"
    93→else
    94→    echo -e "${YELLOW}⚠ Public URL not yet accessible — configure Cloudflare tunnel${NC}"
    95→fi
    96→
    97→echo ""
    98→echo -e "${GREEN}=== Deployment complete ===${NC}"
    99→echo ""
   100→echo "To seed a fresh database:  docker exec $CONTAINER_NAME node dist/server/db/seed.js"
   101→
```

Perfect! I've found the exact SQL queries used to compute both "Teilnehmer" (participants) and "Rapporte" (reports). Here's my analysis:

## Summary of Statistics Queries

There are three main places where these stats are calculated:

### 1. **List All Exercises** - `/api/v1/exercises` (lines 11-18)

**totalParticipants:**
```sql
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM signal_reports 
WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0
```

**totalReports:**
```sql
SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports 
WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL
```

---

### 2. **Exercise Stats Endpoint** - `/api/v1/exercises/:id/stats` (lines 119-181)

**totalParticipants:** (line 122)
```sql
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) as c FROM signal_reports 
WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_op_marker = 0
```

**totalReports:** (line 123)
```sql
SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports 
WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL
```

---

### 3. **Aggregated Reports Stats** - `/api/v1/reports/stats` (lines 47-53 in reports.ts)

**totalParticipants:** (lines 47-49)
```sql
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) as c FROM signal_reports 
WHERE exercise_id IN (...) AND is_op_marker = 0
```

**totalReports:** (lines 51-53)
```sql
SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports 
WHERE exercise_id IN (...) AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL
```

---

## Key Difference That Could Cause Discrepancy

The **fundamental difference** between the two counts is:

- **Participants (Teilnehmer)**: Counts **unique operator IDs** who have ANY signal report (regardless of whether `readability` is filled in)
- **Reports (Rapporte)**: Counts **ALL signal report rows** where `readability IS NOT NULL`

### Why the numbers would differ:

1. **Multiple reports per operator**: An operator can have multiple signal reports across different repeaters or times. If an operator submits 5 different reports, they count as 1 participant but 5 (or fewer if some lack readability) reports.

2. **Incomplete reports**: If a signal report row exists but `readability` is NULL (incomplete entry), it counts the operator as a participant but NOT as a report.

3. **Operator markers**: The filter `is_op_marker = 0` excludes marker/placeholder entries from both counts.

---

## File Locations (Absolute Paths)

- `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/exercises.ts` - Lines 13-14 (list), 122-123 (stats endpoint)
- `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/reports.ts` - Lines 47-53 (aggregated stats)
