Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me provide a comprehensive report on the color definitions used in both map components.

## Report: Color Definitions in AustriaBezirkMap and AustriaMap Components

I've analyzed both map components and found their heatmap color implementations. Here are the exact color values:

### **AustriaBezirkMap Component**
**File:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/AustriaBezirkMap.tsx`

**Heatmap Color Function (lines 22-30):**
```typescript
function getHeatColor(value: number, max: number): string {
  if (max === 0 || value === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
  const t = Math.min(value / max, 1);
  // Green (120) → Yellow (60) → Red (0) via HSL
  const h = 120 * (1 - t);
  const s = 70 + 15 * t; // 70-85%
  const l = 45 + 10 * (1 - t); // 45-55%
  return `hsl(${h}, ${s}%, ${l}%)`;
}
```

**Color Values:**
- **Empty/Zero:** `#f3f4f6` (light gray)
- **HSL Gradient:** Green → Yellow → Red
  - Green (max value): `hsl(120, 70%, 55%)` 
  - Yellow (mid value): `hsl(60, 78%, 50%)`
  - Red (min value): `hsl(0, 85%, 45%)`
- **Stroke (bezirk borders):** `#ffffff` (white)
- **Bundesland borders:** `#1e3a5f` (dark navy)
- **Labels (with data):** `#1e3a5f` (dark navy)
- **Labels (no data):** `#9ca3af` (light gray)

**Legend Gradient (lines 162-172):**
```css
background: 'linear-gradient(to right, hsl(120,70%,55%), hsl(60,78%,50%), hsl(0,85%,45%))'
```

---

### **AustriaMap Component**
**File:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/AustriaMap.tsx`

**Heatmap Color Function (lines 148-156):**
```typescript
function getColor(value: number, max: number): string {
  if (max === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
  if (value === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
  const t = value / max;
  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);
  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);
  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);
  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
}
```

**Color Values:**
- **Empty/Zero:** `#f3f4f6` (light gray, same as Bezirk map)
- **RGB Gradient:** Light Blue → Dark Blue
  - Minimum (value=0): `rgb(194, 213, 236)` (light blue `#c2d5ec`)
  - Maximum (value=max): `rgb(30, 58, 95)` (dark navy `#1e3a5f`)
- **Polygon fill (no data):** `#e5e7eb` (light gray)
- **Polygon stroke:** `#d1d5db` (medium gray)
- **State borders (Bundesländer):** `#1e3a5f` (dark navy)
- **Text (with data):** `#1e3a5f` (dark navy when value/max ≤ 0.5, otherwise `#ffffff` white)
- **Text (no data):** `#9ca3af` (light gray)

**Legend Gradient (line 342):**
```css
background: 'linear-gradient(to right, #c2d5ec, #1e3a5f)'
```

---

### **Summary of Color Differences**

| Aspect | AustriaBezirkMap | AustriaMap |
|--------|------------------|-----------|
| **Empty state** | `#f3f4f6` | `#f3f4f6` |
| **Color mode** | HSL (Green→Yellow→Red) | RGB (Light Blue→Dark Blue) |
| **Min color** | `hsl(120, 70%, 55%)` (green) | `rgb(194, 213, 236)` (#c2d5ec) (light blue) |
| **Max color** | `hsl(0, 85%, 45%)` (red) | `rgb(30, 58, 95)` (#1e3a5f) (dark blue) |
| **Accent colors** | `#1e3a5f`, `#9ca3af`, `#ffffff` | `#1e3a5f`, `#9ca3af`, `#e5e7eb`, `#d1d5db` |

Both components share the same dark navy (`#1e3a5f`) for text labels and state borders, and both use `#f3f4f6` for empty/zero values, but they employ completely different color schemes for their heatmap gradients.

> AGENT

Here's what I found:

## Teilnehmer vs Rapporte — Why Different Numbers

This is **expected behavior**, not a bug:

- **Teilnehmer** = `COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id)` — unique callsigns
- **Rapporte** = `COUNT(*)` where `readability IS NOT NULL` — total report rows

So if operator OE8YML reports on 3 different repeaters, that's **1 Teilnehmer** but **3 Rapporte**. Rapporte will always be >= Teilnehmer since each participant can submit multiple signal reports across different repeaters.

## Heatmap Colors

The two maps use completely different color schemes:
- **AustriaMap** (Bundesland): light blue → dark navy (`#c2d5ec` → `#1e3a5f`) — fits the app's color scheme
- **AustriaBezirkMap** (Bezirk): green → yellow → red (traffic light HSL) — does **not** match

Should I change the Bezirk heatmap to also use the blue gradient to match the app's `#1e3a5f` color scheme? That would make both maps consistent.

> DEVELOPER

```
   119→        {/* Labels for all Bezirke */}
   120→        {Object.entries(BEZIRK_PATHS).map(([code, { labelX, labelY }]) => {
   121→          const bz = dataMap.get(code);
   122→          const hasData = bz && bz.participants > 0;
   123→          const isDark = hasData && bz.participants / maxParticipants > 0.5;
   124→          return (
   125→            <text
   126→              key={`label-${code}`}
   127→              x={labelX}
   128→              y={labelY}
   129→              textAnchor="middle"
   130→              dominantBaseline="central"
   131→              fontSize={hasData ? '6' : '5'}
   132→              fontWeight={hasData ? 'bold' : 'normal'}
   133→              fill={hasData ? (isDark ? '#ffffff' : '#1e3a5f') : '#9ca3af'}
   134→              pointerEvents="none"
   135→              stroke="#ffffff"
   136→              strokeWidth={hasData ? '2' : '1.5'}
   137→              paintOrder="stroke"
   138→            >
   139→              {code}
   140→            </text>
   141→          );
   142→        })}
   143→      </svg>
